My bachelor GPA is 2.7. Does UAEU definitely reject me for every masters program?

Expected answer: No blanket rejection; conditional route GPA>=2.5<3 with max9credits firstsemester and GPA3 in first9credits, subject to program decision and other criteria.

Official UAEU source supporting the expected answer:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

Complete conversation:

Actual localhost API conversation:

User: My bachelor GPA is 2.7. Does UAEU definitely reject me for every masters program?

Assistant:

A bachelor GPA of 2.7 does not lead to automatic rejection for every UAEU masters program; applicants with a GPA at least 2.5 and below 3.0 may qualify for conditional admission subject to other criteria and the university's decision. Under the conditional GPA admission route, a student may take at most 9 credit hours in the first semester and must achieve a GPA of 3.0 in those 9 credit-bearing hours to continue, or else face dismissal.

Chatbot source:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

User: What are UAEU masters admission requirements for GPA and IELTS?

For admission to UAEU masters programs taught predominantly in English, applicants must have a related accredited bachelor degree, a minimum GPA of 3.0 on a 4.0 scale or equivalent, and an IELTS Academic score of at least 6.0 or equivalent, with the language test taken within two years of application. Applicants with a bachelor GPA between 2.5 and below 3.0 may be conditionally admitted, subject to university approval, with restrictions on credit hours and a requirement to achieve a GPA of 3.0 in the first 9 credit hours to continue. Applicants with an IELTS Academic score of 5.5 may be conditionally admitted, required to achieve 6.0 by the end of the first semester, initially take no more than 6 credit hours excluding intensive English, and obtain a GPA of 3.0 in the first 9 credit-bearing hours.

NQ118 — [Missing Knowledge]

Tester name: Codex

Date: 4 November 2026 (Asia/Dubai)

Version tested: Local, http://localhost:3000, branch fix/qa-audit-2026-11-03. Final production API run; runtime 8d396399ba76ea58.

Question or scenario:

IELTS and GPA for masters admission, UAEU?

Expected answer: General CGS minimum bachelorGPA3.0 and IELTS Academic6.0/equivalent, test<2years, exceptions and program-specific standards apply.

Official UAEU source supporting the expected answer:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

Complete conversation:

Actual localhost API conversation:

User: IELTS and GPA for masters admission, UAEU?

Assistant:

For general English-taught masters admission at UAEU, applicants need a related accredited bachelor degree, a GPA of 3.0/4.0 or equivalent, and an IELTS Academic score of 6.0 or equivalent, with the language test being less than two years old at application. Applicants with a bachelor GPA between 2.5 and below 3.0 may qualify for conditional admission, requiring them to take at most 9 credit hours in the first semester and achieve a GPA of 3.0 in those hours to continue. For conditional IELTS admission, applicants with an IELTS Academic score of 5.5 can be admitted conditionally, provided they obtain a 6.0 by the end of the first semester, initially take no more than 6 credit hours excluding intensive English, and achieve a GPA of 3.0 in the first 9 credit-bearing hours.

Chatbot source:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

Question or scenario:

Can a 2.7 bachelor GPA get conditional masters admission?

Expected answer: No blanket rejection; conditional route GPA>=2.5<3 with max9credits firstsemester and GPA3 in first9credits, subject to program decision and other criteria.

Official UAEU source supporting the expected answer:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

Complete conversation:

Actual localhost API conversation:

User: Can a 2.7 bachelor GPA get conditional masters admission?

Assistant:

A bachelor GPA of at least 2.5 and below 3.0, such as 2.7, may qualify for conditional masters admission at UAEU, subject to other criteria and the university's decision. Under this conditional admission route, the student may take at most 9 credit hours in the first semester and must obtain a GPA of 3.0 in those first 9 credit-bearing hours to continue, or else face dismissal. A GPA of 2.7 does not guarantee admission nor automatic rejection; it is evaluated case by case.

Chatbot source:

https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml

At UAEU, student housing is managed through the Rooms Assignment Service, which allows students to select their building and room after enrollment and meeting the distance requirement of living more than 50 km outside Al Ain. To apply for housing, students must sign in using UAE Pass or UAEU credentials, pay the applicable fees (international students must pay before entry), and then select their preferred building and room; confirmation of the assignment is sent by email. The housing application process is immediate and typically completes within 5 minutes, but availability is not guaranteed, and there are no installment plans or fee exemptions available according to the FAQ.
